j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rj = a + j * ld;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rj[k] * rj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return (long)j;\n        }\n        const double d = sqrt(val);\n        rj[j] = d;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < m; ++i) {\n            double* ri = a + i * ld;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s)\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += ri[k] * rj[k];\n            }\n            ri[j] = (ri[j] - s) / d;\n        }\n    }\n    return -1;\n}\n\n// B := B * L^{-T}, where L is the m x m lower-triangular diagonal tile and B is mb x m.\nvoid trsmTile(const double* l, double* b, const size_t ld, const size_t mb, const size_t m) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mb; ++r) {\n        double* br = b + r * ld;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n            const double* lj = l + j * ld;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:s)\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += br[k] * lj[k];\n            }\n            br[j] = (br[j] - s) / lj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C := C - A * B^T, A is mi x kb, B is mj x kb, C is mi x mj (all with leading dim ld).\n// If lowerOnly, only micro-tiles touching the lower triangle of C are updated.\nvoid gemmTile(const double* A, const double* B, double* C, const size_t ld,\n              const size_t mi, const size_t mj, const size_t kb, const bool lowerOnly) {\n    static thread_local std::vector<double> apack, bpack;\n    const size_t mip = (mi + MR - 1) / MR * MR;\n    const size_t mjp = (mj + NR - 1) / NR * NR;\n    if (apack.size() < mip * kb) apack.resize(mip * kb);\n    if (bpack.size() < mjp * kb) bpack.resize(mjp * kb);\n    double* __restrict ap = apack.data();\n    double* __restrict bp = bpack.data();\n\n    // Pack A into MR-row panels: ap[panel][k][r]\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < mip; i0 += MR) {\n        double* dst = ap + i0 * kb;\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + r;\n            if (i < mi) {\n                const double* src = A + i * ld;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = src[k];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Pack B into NR-row panels: bp[panel][k][c]\n    for (size_t j0 = 0; j0 < mjp; j0 += NR) {\n        double* dst = bp + j0 * kb;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) {\n            const size_t j = j0 + c;\n            if (j < mj) {\n                const double* src = B + j * ld;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) dst[k * NR + c] = src[k];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) dst[k * NR + c] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t j0 = 0; j0 < mjp; j0 += NR) {\n        const double* __restrict bpp = bp + j0 * kb;\n        for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < mip; i0 += MR) {\n            if (lowerOnly && i0 + MR <= j0) continue;\n            const double* __restrict app = ap + i0 * kb;\n            double acc[MR][NR] = {};\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n                const double* a = app + k * MR;\n                const double* b = bpp + k * NR;\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n                    #pragma omp simd\n                    for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) {\n                        acc[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            const size_t rmax = std::min(MR, mi - std::min(mi, i0));\n            const size_t cmax = std::min(NR, mj - std::min(mj, j0));\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < rmax; ++r) {\n                double* crow = C + (i0 + r) * ld + j0;\n                for (size_t c = 0; c < cmax; ++c) {\n                    crow[c] -= acc[r][c];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nsize_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n) {\n    const int threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    // Prefer large tiles for kernel efficiency, but keep enough tiles for all threads\n    size_t nb = 192;\n    while (nb > 32) {\n        const size_t nt = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n        if (nt * nt / 2 >= (size_t)threads * 2) break;\n        nb /= 2;\n    }\n    return nb;\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    const size_t nb = chooseBlockSize(n);\n    const size_t nt = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n    double* a = A.data();\n    std::atomic<long> failIndex{-1};\n\n    auto tile = [&](size_t i, size_t j) { return a + i * nb * n + j * nb; };\n    auto tdim = [&](size_t i) { return std::min(nb, n - i * nb); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nt; ++k) {\n            double* akk = tile(k, k);\n            #pragma omp task firstprivate(k, akk) depend(inout: akk[0]) priority(100)\n            {\n                if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0) {\n                    const long f = potrfTile(akk, n, tdim(k));\n                    if (f >= 0) failIndex.store((long)(k * nb) + f);\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nt; ++i) {\n                double* aik = tile(i, k);\n                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, k, akk, aik) depend(in: akk[0]) depend(inout: aik[0]) priority(50)\n                {\n                    if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0)\n                        trsmTile(akk, aik, n, tdim(i), tdim(k));\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nt; ++i) {\n                double* aik = tile(i, k);\n                for (size_t j = k + 1; j <= i; ++j) {\n                    double* ajk = tile(j, k);\n                    double* aij = tile(i, j);\n                    const int prio = (j == k + 1) ? 25 : 0;\n                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, j, k, aik, ajk, aij) depend(in: aik[0], ajk[0]) depend(inout: aij[0]) priority(prio)\n                    {\n                        if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0)\n                            gemmTile(aik, ajk, aij, n, tdim(i), tdim(j), tdim(k), i == j);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    const long f = failIndex.load();\n    if (f >= 0) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", (size_t)f);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute C[i][j] = sum_k X[i*n+k] * X[j*n+k] for all i, j in parallel.\n// Each element is accumulated in the same sequential k order as a plain triple loop.\nstatic void multiplyByTranspose(const std::vector<double>& X, std::vector<double>& C, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t BI = 4, BJ = 4;\n    const double* x = X.data();\n    double* c = C.data();\n    const size_t nbi = (n + BI - 1) / BI;\n    const size_t nbj = (n + BJ - 1) / BJ;\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t bi = 0; r < rmax; ++r) {\n                double* crow = C + (i0 + r) * ld + j0;\n                for (size_t c = 0; c < cmax; ++c) {\n                    crow[c] -= acc[r][c];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nsize_t chooseBlockSize(const size_t n) {\n    const int threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    // Prefer large tiles for kernel efficiency, but keep enough tiles for all threads\n    size_t nb = 192;\n    while (nb > 32) {\n        const size_t nt = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n        if (nt * nt / 2 >= (size_t)threads * 2) break;\n        nb /= 2;\n    }\n    return nb;\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    const size_t nb = chooseBlockSize(n);\n    const size_t nt = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n    double* a = A.data();\n    std::atomic<long> failIndex{-1};\n\n    auto tile = [&](size_t i, size_t j) { return a + i * nb * n + j * nb; };\n    auto tdim = [&](size_t i) { return std::min(nb, n - i * nb); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nt; ++k) {\n            double* akk = tile(k, k);\n            #pragma omp task firstprivate(k, akk) depend(inout: akk[0]) priority(100)\n            {\n                if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0) {\n                    const long f = potrfTile(akk, n, tdim(k));\n                    if (f >= 0) failIndex.store((long)(k * nb) + f);\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nt; ++i) {\n                double* aik = tile(i, k);\n                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, k, akk, aik) depend(in: akk[0]) depend(inout: aik[0]) priority(50)\n                {\n                    if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0)\n                        trsmTile(akk, aik, n, tdim(i), tdim(k));\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nt; ++i) {\n                double* aik = tile(i, k);\n                for (size_t j = k + 1; j <= i; ++j) {\n                    double* ajk = tile(j, k);\n                    double* aij = tile(i, j);\n                    const int prio = (j == k + 1) ? 25 : 0;\n                    #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, j, k, aik, ajk, aij) depend(in: aik[0], ajk[0]) depend(inout: aij[0]) priority(prio)\n                    {\n                        if (failIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < 0)\n                            gemmTile(aik, ajk, aij, n, tdim(i), tdim(j), tdim(k), i == j);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    const long f = failIndex.load();\n    if (f >= 0) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", (size_t)f);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute C[i][j] = sum_k X[i*n+k] * X[j*n+k] for all i, j in parallel.\n// Each element is accumulated in the same sequential k order as a plain triple loop.\nstatic void multiplyByTranspose(const std::vector<double>& X, std::vector<double>& C, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t BI = 4, BJ = 4;\n    const double* x = X.data();\n    double* c = C.data();\n    const size_t nbi = (n + BI - 1) / BI;\n    const size_t nbj = (n + BJ - 1) / BJ;\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t bi = 0; bi < nbi; ++bi) {\n        for (size_t bj = 0; bj < nbj; ++bj) {\n            const size_t i0 = bi * BI, j0 = bj * BJ;\n            if (i0 + BI <= n && j0 + BJ <= n) {\n                double acc[BI][BJ] = {};\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < BI; ++r) {\n                        const double xi = x[(i0 + r) * n + k];\n                        for (size_t s = 0; s < BJ; ++s) {\n                            acc[r][s] += xi * x[(j0 + s) * n + k];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < BI; ++r)\n                    for (size_t s = 0; s < BJ; ++s)\n                        c[(i0 + r) * n + j0 + s] = acc[r][s];\n            } else {\n                for (size_t i = i0; i < std::min(n, i0 + BI); ++i) {\n                    for (size_t j = j0; j < std::min(n, j0 + BJ); ++j) {\n                        double sum = 0.0;\n                        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                            sum += x[i * n + k] * x[j * n + k];\n                        }\n                        c[i * n + j] = sum;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\nsrc=open('cholesky.cpp').read()\ntop=open('/tmp/new_top.cpp').read()\ni=src.index('// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix')\nrest=src[i:]\nold_gen='''    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rj = a + j * ld;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n\n} // namespace\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    // Panel width (inner dimension of the trailing updates) and update chunk size\n    const size_t nb = n >= 6144 ? 192 : (n >= 3072 ? 128 : 96);\n    const size_t cb = nb;\n    double* a = A.data();\n    long failIndex = -1;\n    // Small problems do not have enough work per step to feed every thread\n    const int nthreads = (int)std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(), n / 32));\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n    {\n        // Factor the first diagonal block\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            const long f = potrfTile(a, n, std::min(nb, n));\n            if (f >= 0) failIndex = f;\n        }\n\n        std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>> chunks;\n        for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < n; k0 += nb) {\n            // Barrier above guarantees failIndex and diagonal block k0 are final\n            if (failIndex >= 0) break;\n            const size_t kb = std::min(nb, n - k0);\n            const size_t r0 = k0 + kb;  // first row of the trailing matrix\n            if (r0 >= n) break;\n            const double* akk = a + k0 * n + k0;\n\n            // Panel solve: rows below the diagonal block are independent\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = r0; i < n; ++i) {\n                trsmTile(akk, a + i * n + k0, n, 1, kb);\n            }\n\n            // Trailing update: A22 -= L21 * L21^T (lower triangle only).\n            // Chunk 0 is the next diagonal block, which is factored right after its\n            // update (lookahead), overlapping with the remaining update chunks.\n            const size_t kb2 = std::min(nb, n - r0);\n            const size_t r1 = r0 + kb2;\n            chunks.clear();\n            chunks.emplace_back(r0, r0);\n            for (size_t i0 = r1; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfjVcfT3uCodc8o3LuGhy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The Cholesky benchmark now runs in parallel with OpenMP. At n=2048 the timed factorization drops from 2133 ms to about 24 ms (~90×) on this 2×64-core EPYC. All validation runs pass, but timings are noisy because other users' jobs had the machine heavily loaded (load average up to ~160).\n\n| n | Original | New (256 threads available) |\n|---|---|---|\n| 512 | — | 11 ms |\n| 2048 | 2133 ms | 24 ms |\n| 4096 | — | 89 ms |\n| 8192 | ~16.8 s single-threaded (new code, 1 thread) | 332 ms |\n\n**What changed in `cholesky/cholesky.cpp`:**\n- **Algorithm:** the factorization is now a blocked version that works in one parallel region:\n  - Each step factors a diagonal block, then solves the rows below it in parallel.\n  - The rest of the matrix is then updated in parallel chunks, with threads taking chunks as they free up.\n  - The first chunk of each update also factors the next diagonal block, so that serial step overlaps with the rest of the update.\n- **Fast inner kernel:** a 6×8 register-blocked matrix-multiply kernel using GCC vector extensions. Single-threaded it reaches about 28 real GFLOP/s.\n- **Tuning:** block size depends on n (96, 128 or 192). Thread count is capped at n/32, because small matrices otherwise lose time to synchronisation overhead.\n- **Upper-triangle zeroing** runs inside the same parallel region. As a separate region it cost about 45 ms at n=512 with 256 threads.\n- **Matrix generation and validation** (both untimed) are also parallel. Each entry is still summed in the original order, so the generated input matrix is bit-identical to the original's (checked by comparing output at n=333 and n=1024).\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable is still named `cholesky`.\n\n**Correctness:**\n- `-v` validation passes for n = 1, 2, 5, 97, 191, 500, 1537, 2048 and 3001.\n- With `-r`, the output matches the original's statistics to about 1e-15 relative. The hash differs, because the factorization adds things up in a different order.\n- The maximum relative error at some sizes is larger than before (about 2e-9 vs 2e-12 at n=2048), still far below the 1e-6 tolerance. This comes from entries of the original matrix that are close to zero; the absolute error is unchanged at ~2e-12.\n- For a matrix that isn't positive definite, it reports the same failing diagonal index as the original. I tested this by forcing negative diagonal entries at 0, 95, 96, 300 and 511 with a temporary test build.\n\n**Why not task dependencies:** I tried a task-graph version first, but at 256 threads it was slower than the original. Contention on the OpenMP runtime's single shared task queue made n=2048 take about 4.7 s, so I replaced it.\n\n**Scaling limit:** at n=2048, 64 threads were often as fast as or faster than 128–256 in these tests. That reflects both per-step synchronisation cost and the other load on the machine, so a quiet machine may scale better."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":528,"cache_read_input_tokens":63012,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":528},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"095f9ccc-41bc-40a2-b738-f3599117b49f","uuid":"c30fe3fe-c68a-455e-9bc6-e22777a9c4c0","timestamp":"2026-10-05T17:35:48.956Z","request_id":"req_011CfjVcewYz5FphVdWH7RLe"}
